


<div align="center">

<samp> 
  Google Authenticator should not exist. Easily export OTP codes with <a href="https://github.com/dim13/otpauth">otpauth</a> and migrate today. <br><br>
</samp>

<samp> 
To remove cli telemetry use https://github.com/alloydwhitlock/do-not-track-cli ( this will not save you from Google's telemetry on mediapipe or others ) 
</samp>

<br><br>
